Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in calculus theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 788 and the invariant I(A,B) = 459 mod 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_788(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 788. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^459(S, F) is trivial. QED.
